{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Shared LoRA injection module, extracted from train_a3_hook.py.\\n\\nProvides manual LoRA (low-rank adaptation) without PEFT dependency.\\nLoRALinear wraps nn.Linear with frozen base weights + trainable lora_A/lora_B.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\nTARGET_MODULES = (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                  \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        _device = base.weight.device\\n        _dtype = base.weight.dtype\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank, device=_device, dtype=_dtype) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f, device=_device, dtype=_dtype))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    @property\\n    def weight(self):\\n        return self.base.weight\\n\\n    @property\\n    def bias(self):\\n        return self.base.bias\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        base_out = self.base(x.to(self.base.weight.dtype))\\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A.float() @ self.lora_B.float()) * self.scaling\\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora(module, lora_rank=16, lora_alpha=16.0, target_modules=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Inject LoRA adapters into matching nn.Linear layers within module.\\n\\n    Walks all sub-modules. For any nn.Linear whose attribute name matches\\n    an entry in target_modules, replaces it with LoRALinear.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        module: nn.Module to inject LoRA into.\\n        lora_rank: Rank of the LoRA decomposition.\\n        lora_alpha: Scaling factor (effective scale = alpha / rank).\\n        target_modules: List of attribute names to replace.\\n            Defaults to TARGET_MODULES (q/k/v/o_proj + gate/up/down_proj).\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of LoRA parameters (lora_A, lora_B pairs) for the optimizer.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if target_modules is None:\\n        target_modules = list(TARGET_MODULES)\\n\\n    lora_params = []\\n    for name, child in list(module.named_modules()):\\n        if not name:\\n            continue\\n        parts = name.split(\\\".\\\")\\n        attr_name = parts[-1]\\n        if attr_name in target_modules and isinstance(child, nn.Linear):\\n            parent_path = \\\".\\\".join(parts[:-1])\\n            parent = module.get_submodule(parent_path) if parent_path else module\\n            lora_mod = LoRALinear(child, rank=lora_rank, alpha=lora_alpha)\\n            setattr(parent, attr_name, lora_mod)\\n            lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\\n\\n    return lora_params\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora_pi0(model, pali_rank=16, pali_alpha=16.0,\\n                    expert_rank=32, expert_alpha=32.0, target_modules=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Pi0-specific LoRA injection: separate rank/alpha for backbone vs expert.\\n\\n    PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) -> pali_rank/pali_alpha\\n    Action Expert (Gemma 300M) -> expert_rank/expert_alpha\\n\\n    Args:\\n        model: Pi0 model (unwrapped, not DDP).\\n        pali_rank: LoRA rank for PaliGemma backbone.\\n        pali_alpha: LoRA alpha for PaliGemma backbone.\\n        expert_rank: LoRA rank for action expert.\\n        expert_alpha: LoRA alpha for action expert.\\n        target_modules: Override target module names.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (all_lora_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pali_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model\\n    expert_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model\\n\\n    pali_lora_params = inject_lora(pali_model, lora_rank=pali_rank,\\n                                   lora_alpha=pali_alpha,\\n                                   target_modules=target_modules)\\n    expert_lora_params = inject_lora(expert_model, lora_rank=expert_rank,\\n                                     lora_alpha=expert_alpha,\\n                                     target_modules=target_modules)\\n\\n    all_params = pali_lora_params + expert_lora_params\\n    logging.info(\\\"LoRA injected: PaliGemma rank=%d (%d params), Expert rank=%d (%d params)\\\",\\n                 pali_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in pali_lora_params),\\n                 expert_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in expert_lora_params))\\n    return all_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.15}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Shared LoRA injection module, extracted from train_a3_hook.py.\\n\\nProvides manual LoRA (low-rank adaptation) without PEFT dependency.\\nLoRALinear wraps nn.Linear with frozen base weights + trainable lora_A/lora_B.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\nTARGET_MODULES = (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                  \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        _device = base.weight.device\\n        _dtype = base.weight.dtype\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank, device=_device, dtype=_dtype) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f, device=_device, dtype=_dtype))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    @property\\n    def weight(self):\\n        return self.base.weight\\n\\n    @property\\n    def bias(self):\\n        return self.base.bias\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        base_out = self.base(x.to(self.base.weight.dtype))\\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A.float() @ self.lora_B.float()) * self.scaling\\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora(module, lora_rank=16, lora_alpha=16.0, target_modules=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Inject LoRA adapters into matching nn.Linear layers within module.\\n\\n    Walks all sub-modules. For any nn.Linear whose attribute name matches\\n    an entry in target_modules, replaces it with LoRALinear.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        module: nn.Module to inject LoRA into.\\n        lora_rank: Rank of the LoRA decomposition.\\n        lora_alpha: Scaling factor (effective scale = alpha / rank).\\n        target_modules: List of attribute names to replace.\\n            Defaults to TARGET_MODULES (q/k/v/o_proj + gate/up/down_proj).\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of LoRA parameters (lora_A, lora_B pairs) for the optimizer.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if target_modules is None:\\n        target_modules = list(TARGET_MODULES)\\n\\n    lora_params = []\\n    for name, child in list(module.named_modules()):\\n        if not name:\\n            continue\\n        parts = name.split(\\\".\\\")\\n        attr_name = parts[-1]\\n        if attr_name in target_modules and isinstance(child, nn.Linear):\\n            parent_path = \\\".\\\".join(parts[:-1])\\n            parent = module.get_submodule(parent_path) if parent_path else module\\n            lora_mod = LoRALinear(child, rank=lora_rank, alpha=lora_alpha)\\n            setattr(parent, attr_name, lora_mod)\\n            lora_params.extend([lora_mod.lora_A, lora_mod.lora_B])\\n\\n    return lora_params\\n\\n\\ndef inject_lora_pi0(model, pali_rank=16, pali_alpha=16.0,\\n                    expert_rank=32, expert_alpha=32.0, target_modules=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Pi0-specific LoRA injection: separate rank/alpha for backbone vs expert.\\n\\n    PaliGemma (Gemma 2B) -> pali_rank/pali_alpha\\n    Action Expert (Gemma 300M) -> expert_rank/expert_alpha\\n\\n    Args:\\n        model: Pi0 model (unwrapped, not DDP).\\n        pali_rank: LoRA rank for PaliGemma backbone.\\n        pali_alpha: LoRA alpha for PaliGemma backbone.\\n        expert_rank: LoRA rank for action expert.\\n        expert_alpha: LoRA alpha for action expert.\\n        target_modules: Override target module names.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        (all_lora_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    pali_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model\\n    expert_model = model.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model\\n\\n    pali_lora_params = inject_lora(pali_model, lora_rank=pali_rank,\\n                                   lora_alpha=pali_alpha,\\n                                   target_modules=target_modules)\\n    expert_lora_params = inject_lora(expert_model, lora_rank=expert_rank,\\n                                     lora_alpha=expert_alpha,\\n                                     target_modules=target_modules)\\n\\n    all_params = pali_lora_params + expert_lora_params\\n    logging.info(\\\"LoRA injected: PaliGemma rank=%d (%d params), Expert rank=%d (%d params)\\\",\\n                 pali_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in pali_lora_params),\\n                 expert_rank, sum(p.numel() for p in expert_lora_params))\\n    return all_params, pali_lora_params, expert_lora_params\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.15}}"

Backward pass\\n            loss.backward()\\n\\n            # Log memory usage after backward pass\\n            if global_step < 5 and is_main and torch.cuda.is_available():\\n                log_memory_usage(device, global_step, \\\"after_backward\\\")\\n\\n            # Gradient clipping\\n            clip_params = list(model.parameters())\\n            if hooks is not None:\\n                clip_params = clip_params + list(hooks.extra_params())\\n            grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(clip_params, max_norm=config.optimizer.clip_gradient_norm)\\n\\n            # Optimizer step\\n            optim.step()\\n            optim.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)\\n\\n            # Clear gradients more aggressively\\n            for param in model.parameters():\\n                if param.grad is not None:\\n                    param.grad.detach_()\\n                    param.grad = None\\n\\n            # Collect stats\\n            if is_main:\\n                step_info = {\\n                    \\\"loss\\\": loss.item(),\\n                    \\\"learning_rate\\\": optim.param_groups[0][\\\"lr\\\"],\\n                    \\\"grad_norm\\\": float(grad_norm) if isinstance(grad_norm, torch.Tensor) else grad_norm,\\n                }\\n                step_info.update(aux_info)\\n                infos.append(step_info)\\n\\n            if is_main and (global_step % config.log_interval == 0):\\n                elapsed = time.time() - start_time\\n\\n                # Average stats over log interval\\n                avg_loss = sum(info[\\\"loss\\\"] for info in infos) / len(infos)\\n                avg_lr = sum(info[\\\"learning_rate\\\"] for info in infos) / len(infos)\\n\\n                avg_grad_norm = None\\n                if any(\\\"grad_norm\\\" in info for info in infos):\\n                    vals = [\\n                        info[\\\"grad_norm\\\"] for info in infos if \\\"grad_norm\\\" in info and info[\\\"grad_norm\\\"] is not None\\n                    ]\\n                    if len(vals) > 0:\\n                        avg_grad_norm = sum(vals) / len(vals)\\n                logging.info(\\n                    f\\\"step={global_step} loss={avg_loss:.4f} lr={avg_lr:.2e} grad_norm={avg_grad_norm:.2f} time={elapsed:.1f}s\\\"\\n                    if avg_grad_norm is not None\\n                    else f\\\"step={global_step} loss={avg_loss:.4f} lr={avg_lr:.2e} time={elapsed:.1f}s\\\"\\n                )\\n\\n                # Log to wandb\\n                if config.wandb_enabled and len(infos) > 0:\\n                    log_payload = {\\n                        \\\"loss\\\": avg_loss,\\n                        \\\"learning_rate\\\": avg_lr,\\n                        \\\"step\\\": global_step,\\n                        \\\"time_per_step\\\": elapsed / config.log_interval,\\n                    }\\n                    if avg_grad_norm is not None:\\n                        log_payload[\\\"grad_norm\\\"] = avg_grad_norm\\n                    if hooks is not None and infos:\\n                        for _k in infos[0]:\\n                            if _k not in (\\\"loss\\\", \\\"learning_rate\\\", \\\"grad_norm\\\") and isinstance(infos[0].get(_k), (int, float)):\\n                                _vals = [_i[_k] for _i in infos if _k in _i]\\n                                if _vals:\\n                                    log_payload[_k] = sum(_vals) / len(_vals)\\n                    wandb.log(log_payload, step=global_step)\\n\\n                start_time = time.time()\\n                infos = []  # Reset stats collection\\n\\n            global_step += 1\\n            # Save checkpoint using the new mechanism\\n            save_checkpoint(model, optim, global_step, config, is_main, data_config, hooks=hooks, loss=loss.item())\\n\\n            # Update progress bar\\n            if pbar is not None:\\n                pbar.update(1)\\n                pbar.set_postfix(\\n                    {\\\"loss\\\": f\\\"{loss.item():.4f}\\\", \\\"lr\\\": f\\\"{optim.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e}\\\", \\\"step\\\": global_step}\\n                )\\n\\n    # Close progress bar\\n    if pbar is not None:\\n        pbar.close()\\n\\n    if hooks is not None:\\n        hooks.on_cleanup()\\n\\n    # Finish wandb run\\n    if is_main and config.wandb_enabled:\\n        wandb.finish()\\n\\n    cleanup_ddp()\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    init_logging()\\n    config = _config.cli()\\n    train_loop(config)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Shared LoRA injection module, extracted from train_a3_hook.py.\\n\\nProvides manual LoRA (low-rank adaptation) without PEFT dependency.\\nLoRALinear wraps nn.Linear with frozen base weights + trainable lora_A/lora_B.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\nTARGET_MODULES = (\\\"q_proj\\\", \\\"k_proj\\\", \\\"v_proj\\\", \\\"o_proj\\\",\\n                  \\\"gate_proj\\\", \\\"up_proj\\\", \\\"down_proj\\\")\\n\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        _device = base.weight.device\\n        _dtype = base.weight.dtype\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank, device=_device, dtype=_dtype) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f, device=_device, dtype=_dtype))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    @property\\n    def weight(self):\\n        return self.base.weight\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}
```

{
  "server": "westd-45947",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cat > /tmp/patch_tqdm.py << 'PYEOF'\nfpath = \"/root/colliforce-vla/openpi/scripts/train_pytorch.py\"\nwith open(fpath) as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\nold = '''            if pbar is not None:\n                pbar.update(1)\n                pbar.set_postfix(\n                    {\"loss\": f\"{loss.item():.4f}\", \"lr\": f\"{optim.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e}\", \"step\": global_step}\n                )'''\n\nnew = '''            if pbar is not None:\n                pbar.update(1)\n                postfix = {\"loss\": f\"{loss.item():.4f}\", \"lr\": f\"{optim.param_groups[0]['lr']:.2e}\"}\n                if aux_info:\n                    for _ak in (\"action_loss\", \"sdf_total\", \"ee_loss\"):\n                        if _ak in aux_info:\n                            postfix[_ak.replace(\"_loss\",\"\").replace(\"_total\",\"\")] = f\"{aux_info[_ak]:.4f}\"\n                pbar.set_postfix(postfix)'''\n\nif old in content:\n    content = content.replace(old, new)\n    with open(fpath, 'w') as f:\n        f.write(content)\n    print(\"OK: patched set_postfix\")\nelse:\n    print(\"ERROR: old string not found\")\n    lines = content.split('\\n')\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n        if 'set_postfix' in line:\n            print(f\"Line {i+1}: {line}\")\nPYEOF\npython /tmp/patch_tqdm.py",
  "account": "root"
}

---

## D011 Phase 0 结果

### 1. lora_utils.py 同步
**已同步。** westd-35807 缺少 dtype 修复 — lora_A/lora_B 未指定 `dtype=_dtype`，会默认创建 float32 参数（base weight 是 bfloat16）。已用 westd-45947 的修复版覆盖：
- `_dtype = base.weight.dtype` 添加到 `__init__`
- `torch.randn(..., dtype=_dtype)` 和 `torch.zeros(..., dtype=_dtype)`

### 2. Loss logging 修改

**修改文件：** `/root/colliforce-vla/openpi/scripts/train_pytorch.py`（两台服务器）

**A) tqdm postfix**（行 707-712）：添加 `action`, `sdf`, `ee` 三个 loss 组件到每步显示。

**B) W&B**：已有泛化逻辑（遍历 aux_info 所有数值 key 取平均后 wandb.log），无需额外修改。W&B 自动记录：`action_loss`, `sdf_total`, `sdf_l1`, `ee_loss`, `phase`, `eikonal`。

**额外修复：** `logging.basicConfig` 添加 `force=True` 解决 logging.info 被上游模块吞掉的问题（两台服务器的 `train_a1_sdf_aux_pi05.py`）。

### 3. Phase 2 dry-run 结果

在 westd-45947:cuda:0 运行 7 步，`--warmup_phase_steps 2`：

| Step (0-idx) | Phase | total_loss | action_loss | sdf_total | ee_loss |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | 1 | 0.1296 | 0.1257 | 0.1355 | 0.3265 |
| 1 | 1 | 0.1153 | 0.1107 | 0.1376 | 0.3951 |
| 2 | **2** | **0.1525** | 0.1481 | 0.1435 | 0.3639 |
| 3 | 2 | 0.1424 | 0.1382 | 0.1321 | 0.3514 |
| 4 | 2 | 0.1252 | 0.1211 | 0.1267 | 0.3531 |
| 5 | 2 | 0.1193 | 0.1147 | 0.1306 | 0.3925 |
| 6 | 2 | 0.1445 | 0.1403 | 0.1302 | 0.3567 |

- **Phase 1→2 切换确认**：Step 2 total_loss 跳升 0.1153→0.1525（LoRA unfreeze 扰动），符合预期
- **Step 0 trainable params: 658,948，LoRA params: 0**（Phase 1 冻结 LoRA 确认）
- **无 dtype crash，无 NaN**
- GPU 显存已释放，训练正常完成
- Dry-run checkpoint 已清理

### 4. SDF GT 数据

| Scene | 文件 | query_points | sdf_values | range |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| spatial_bowl | `sdf_gt_spatial_bowl.npz` | (4076, 3) | (4076,) | [-0.05, 0.05] |
| object_pudding | `sdf_gt_object_chocolate.npz` | (4084, 3) | (4084,) | [-0.05, 0.05] |

**两个 scene 都有 SDF GT 数据**，两台服务器均存在。

### 5. warmup_phase_steps 确认
代码默认值仍为 `5000`（行 91），dry-run 通过 CLI `--warmup_phase_steps 2` 传参，代码未改动，无需恢复。
